# Implementation backlog

**Mandatory execution entry point: [PLAN.md](../PLAN.md).** Choose work only through that plan and TEAM_PLAN.md assignments. This file is the sole registry for task statuses, dependencies, claims, acceptance criteria and verification evidence.

Version 1.0 • 5 November 2026. **All application tasks are TODO; none are implemented by this documentation delivery.** Implementation requires user approval. P0 blocks MVP release, P1 is a strong enhancement, P2 is deferred. S≈one bounded small change, M≈several coordinated modules, L≈complex integration/research work; these are relative effort, not day estimates. Dependency IDs refer to verified task completion; a forward numeric dependency is intentional where a later epic supplies a service. See [roadmap](IMPLEMENTATION_ROADMAP.md).

Every task includes description, priority, dependencies, implementation steps, expected modules, acceptance, tests, complexity and status. Steps listed with semicolons are sequential within that task. Before starting, read its epic specification and the relevant API/schema/security contracts. Mark BLOCKED only with an explicit missing input/dependency; mark DONE only after all acceptance and required checks pass. No P1/P2 work displaces an uncompleted P0 critical path.

**Description:** Turn landing into an evidence-bearing introduction with source-qualified real values.

**Implementation steps:** Select at most3 meaningful metrics; fetch shared analytical services; add for/against findings with CL citations; implement unavailable/stale fallback and workspace/research CTAs.

**Expected files/modules:** apps/web/src/app/(public)/page.tsx; features/public-research/

**Acceptance criteria:** Every quantitative landing value links definition/dataset; archive dates visible; no live badge without refresh evidence; CTAs reach correct destinations.

**Testing requirements:** API-to-render integration against saved approved input; unavailable-source case; claim citation trace and responsive review.

**Implementation record (2026-11-08, this claim):**
- **Selected metrics (3 of the registry):** `observed_near_purchases` v1 (availability A, SRC-BQ), `treasury_payouts_near` v1 (availability A, SRC-BQ) and `provider_gross_fees_usd` v1 (availability P, SRC-LLAMA). Rationale: together they carry the mechanism stages the thesis actually measures — the core observed conversion quantity (subject of the supporting CL-001 and the contrary CL-002), the matched cash-out leg (CL-003, a different flow never added to purchases) and the explicitly gross, provider-reported, revisable fee leg that must never be relabeled as protocol revenue. All three map to the landing's for/against selection; no fourth metric is needed and the gated revenue metrics stay absent.
- **Fetch:** `apps/web/src/features/public-research/landing-metrics.ts` is a server-only module (used only by the `(public)/page.tsx` server component; imports `loadServerEnv`/`API_INTERNAL_URL`, no client bundle path). It calls the implemented NI-032 route `GET /api/metrics/{metric_id}/series` with `definition_version=1`, `dataset_version=latest`, `frequency=day` and the fixed recorded window `2026-08-06..2026-11-04` (the saved 90-day cohort — the same window the claim registry scopes; a trailing wall-clock window was rejected as silently time-dependent). Responses are runtime-validated with a Zod mirror of the generated `SeriesResponse` contract; invalid payloads become unavailable states, never best-effort renders. Decimal strings are rendered verbatim — nothing is summed, rounded or passed through `Number` (the only derived quantity is a non-financial count of periods holding a value). The page is `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"`: approved-dataset resolution is a per-request read through the API's own ≤60 s latest-pointer cache; a statically prerendered landing could show unavailability while approved data exists.
- **Fallbacks (existing NI-009 data-state components):** 503 DATA_NOT_READY → `UnavailableState` ("this is not zero"); unreachable API / non-JSON / contract-invalid payload → typed unavailable states with recorded reasons; approved dataset covering no period with a value → `EmptyState` with retained units/period metadata; `stale` quality on the latest point or dataset → `StaleNotice` with the last verified values kept visible; explicit per-point gaps stay gaps ("N of M periods hold a value; the rest are explicit gaps, never zeros"). No zeros, no placeholder digits.
- **Live-badge policy:** the landing never renders a live badge. The saved evidence carries no recorded freshness guarantee (`freshness_target_seconds` is null in the MVP contract), so each card renders "Data as of: <source_as_of ?? retrieved_at> (recorded archive timestamp, UTC)" — archive dates are visible; a fresh retrieval never makes an old observation fresh. The tests enforce this structurally (no element presents itself as a live indicator).
- **For/against findings:** `landing-findings.ts` selects supporting CL-001 + CL-003 and contrary CL-002 + CL-007 from the NI-014 claim registry (consumption only — registry entries untouched) and renders them through the shared `ClaimCard`, each side linking into the owning thesis section (`/research#revenue-capture`, `/research#token-economics`). Every rendered claim keeps its exact quantities, scopes, sources, definition/dataset version references and cautions. New stable landing anchors `/#evidence` and `/#findings` are recorded in INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE.md.
- **CTAs:** header links (unchanged `/research`, `/methodology`) plus "Open the workspace" → `/workspace` in the header list and the closing "Continue" section; `/workspace` is the gated shell whose layout redirects unauthenticated visits to `/login` (NI-021) — the CTA text states this honestly. No unimplemented route is linked.
- **Recorded handoff to role B (integration):** the web-level "API-to-render integration against saved approved input" is covered by a fixture shaped exactly like the implemented NI-032 `SeriesResponse` contract (decimal strings, Z-suffixed UTC timestamps, per-point quality, approved dataset projection) driven through the real fetch → validate → render path in `tests/ui/landing-metrics.test.tsx`. The remaining integration evidence — the same rendered landing resolving values from a staged approved dataset through a live FastAPI + PostgreSQL instance — needs the cluster runtime and is a handoff to role B for the cluster integration pass (same pattern as NI-014's publications handoff).

- **Implementation:** Storage is a new owner-scoped metadata table `app.document_organization` (migration `0014_document_organization`, down_revision `0012_export_jobs` per the coordinator's linearization note — 0013 stays reserved for the parallel task): one optional row per document, composite FK `(document_id, owner_id) → documents(id, owner_id)` ON DELETE CASCADE, FORCE RLS with the 0002/0011 policy pair (near_app self policies paired with `app.identity_is_active()`, operator purge-only DELETE, column-specific INSERT/UPDATE grants), tags `text[]` + `favorite boolean` bounded by the DB CHECK `app.is_valid_tag_set` (≤20 tags, 1–40 chars, trim+lower normalized, duplicate-free — re-validated even for raw SQL). Documented tag normalization: trim → lowercase → duplicate-collapse preserving first occurrence. New service module `apps/api/src/near_api/documents/organization.py` (normalize_tags, get/update with PATCH semantics via `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE ... RETURNING`); `PATCH /api/documents/{document_id}/organization` (operation_id `updateDocumentOrganization`, no If-Match — metadata is not CAS-protected content); `GET /documents` grows repeatable `tag` (AND semantics, max 5 → 422 beyond) and `favorite=true|false` filters composed with `q` and pagination inside one owner-scoped `scoped_page` query (LEFT JOIN on the metadata PK — no row multiplication, count stays owner-scoped). **Recorded ordering decision: organization writes never UPDATE `app.documents`** — no revision, no `content_sha256`/`document_versions` change, `documents.updated_at` and the `(updated_at DESC, id)` list order are stable under tagging (tested); the metadata row's own trigger-maintained `updated_at` (0011 pattern, fires only on real change) is response bookkeeping only. Typed client regenerated (`packages/shared-types/openapi.json` + generated `api.ts`); gateway allowlist extended for the `organization` PATCH segment (list query params flow through `forwardPrivate` unchanged). **Description:** Demonstrate a genuinely regularly updated series with last-good reliability.

**Implementation steps:** Implement verified finalized-header or other approved low-cost adapter; enforce allowlist/budgets/locks/checkpoints; validate/publish new version; schedule opt-in; display source freshness and failure.

**Expected files/modules:** apps/api/src/near_api/ingestion/providers/; cli.py; deployment timer config

**Acceptance criteria:** At least one real approved refresh publishes valid data;429/auth failure retains last good; startup defaults disabled; legacy collectors remain stopped.

**Testing requirements:** Controlled HTTP failure/schema/pagination tests; bounded authorized live smoke and recorded fresh block hash/time; scheduler overlap test.

**Result:** `apps/api/src/near_api/ingestion/providers/finalized_header.py` — the single-shot adapter: OQ-03 budgets (1 request/minute, ≤1440/day) evaluated purely and enforced from persisted migration-0008 state before any network I/O; the endpoint comes only from the operator-managed `app.sources` registry (`access_status='approved'`, clean public HTTPS) — the code carries the source identifier, never the URL; `STOP_PUBLIC_COLLECTION` root/research markers gate the adapter exactly like the NI-026 probe; one streamed POST `block finality=final` per invocation with a 65 536-byte cap, identity-encoding rule, 10 s timeout, no redirects and no retry; strict sanitization through the probe's `FinalizedHeader` model (monotonic height, base58 hash, digit-string timestamp/supply); the persisted height checkpoint makes a lower advertised height a `height_regression` failure, an unchanged height publish nothing, and a higher height stage+publish dataset `near-finalized-headers` (metric `total_supply_near`, exact yoctoNEAR→NEAR via a precision-safe local context, raw response preserved as the source-qualified input artifact, revision delta recorded) in the same transaction that advances the checkpoint and counters — any failure retains the previous approved pointer and records `last_failure_at`/`last_failure_kind`/`consecutive_failures`; a failed or schema-invalid response is honestly counted as a consumed attempt. A session-level per-source advisory lock refuses scheduler overlap. `apps/api/src/near_api/ingestion/refresh.py` — the operator CLI (`python -m near_api.ingestion.refresh --once --database-url --storage-root [--repo-root] [--source]`) with exit codes 0/3/4/5 and JSON reports; nothing else imports or starts the adapter (no startup path, no HTTP handler). `apps/api/alembic/versions/0008_refresh_checkpoints.py` — the operator-owned checkpoint/counter zone: FORCE RLS with a single near_operator write policy, near_app read-only for freshness display, no DELETE grant for any role, cursor-pair and non-negative-check constraints. Tests: `apps/api/tests/test_refresh_adapter.py` (offline budget/supply/validation/failure/stop-marker/allowlist classes + 16 database tests) and the env-gated `apps/api/tests/test_refresh_live_smoke.py` (excluded from CI discovery; runs one real request only under `NI_ISOLATED_PG_TEST=1` + `NI_LIVE_SMOKE=1`, which `scripts/run-db-tests.py` forwards). Docs: DATA_PIPELINE.md records the implemented refresh leg and DEPLOYMENT.md the timer opt-in contract. Limitations: only `SRC-NEAR-RPC` header refresh is implemented; the freshness/failure display API belongs to NI-032 (the near_app read grants are already in place); the timer itself is operator infrastructure, not repo code; a single live smoke does not establish an SLA or a numeric provider quota. PR not merged; DONE awaits role-A review.

chart bindings pin exact metric definition versions). `datasets/delivery.py` implements server-side fan-out: a multi-value selection resolves one exact-match compared series per selected value combination in one ChartView (canonical option order, duplicates collapsed, ≤32 series, 422 FILTER_UNSUPPORTED for unknown key/value), the view-level selection and context hash capture the exact multi-selection (single-value selections hash exactly as the pre-NI-071 string form), and a full-universe selection on a reconcilable dimension is checked per period at exact Decimal precision against the declared aggregate definition of the SAME dataset version — a mismatch becomes explicit unavailable points with a reason, never a silent average/sum/pick. `schemas/chart_selection.py` canonicalizes every persisted selection to list values in the declared option order (legacy bare strings normalize on load, so stored saved charts/insights/revisions keep loading and hashing identically); `documents/context_resolver.py` canonicalizes document-context hashes the same way and compares reconstruction metric sets with fan-out dedup; `exports/markdown.py` `_context_lines` now prints the resolved filters; `charts/saved.py`/`exports/ir.py` round-trip the validated selection unchanged. Web: new `apps/web/src/features/charts/filters/` (`filter-values.ts` canonical helpers + `FilterControls.tsx` shared controls — single-select for single-value dimensions, accessible checkbox comparison group for `multi_value` dimensions) rendered by both the chart detail and the editor live-chart filter edit (draft-local until Apply); `selection-url.ts` parses/serializes multi-value lists with honest per-key drop notes and stable canonical URLs (one selected value serializes as the bare string); `catalog-api.ts`/`ChartView.tsx`/`chart-api.ts`/`observation-context.ts` carry the union form with canonical comparison keys; `option-builder.ts` distinguishes compared fan-out series of one metric in legend/tooltip/table by their selected values; saved-chart/home/explorer types regenerated in `packages/shared-types` from the FastAPI OpenAPI.
- **Design decision (server fan-out):** one ChartView resolves N compared series server-side (option (a) of the brief) instead of K independent client queries — one coherent series table/legend/CSV, one context hash and one reconciliation check per selection; per-series context and the URL selection still capture the exact multi-selection, and the renderer/option-builder/SeriesTable already render N series. Recorded in docs/CHART_SYSTEM.md "Query/render contract".
- **Data-gate limitation (honest):** no real dataset was rebuilt. `treasury-payouts` v2 registers now and resolves against current datasets, whose payout rows carry `dimensions={}` — its per-recipient series therefore report explicit missing/unavailable points (never zeros or fabricated values) until an operator rebuilds the dataset from the permitted archive (NI-030/NI-051–054 handoff rules; no collector restart, no bulk collection). The reconciliation identity and per-recipient comparison are proven against synthetic staged datasets in the test suites only.
- **Verification (2026-11-12):** full DB suite `uv run --project apps/api python scripts/run-db-tests.py --pg-bin /opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@18/bin`: **exit 0, 470 tests OK, 0 skipped** across all patterns, including the new NI-071 suites — registry v2 chart/definition + closed fail-closed schema rejections; multi-value parse/validate bounds, canonical order and context-hash stability/legacy equality; per-recipient synthetic dataset views with one series per recipient, exact decimal sum reconciliation (sum of the three recipient series == v1 aggregate per period) and explicit mismatch withholding; FILTER_UNSUPPORTED for unknown/unsupported keys and values; save→insight→export IR round trip (byte-identical double build, insight context carrying the exact selection, markdown filters line); v1 chart views unchanged; invariants oracle extended with the per-recipient storage reconciliation identity. Web: lint clean, strict typecheck clean, production build OK, **536 tests passed (50 files)** including the new NI-071 suites (selection-URL multi-value parse/serialize round trip, drop notes, canonical order; chart-detail comparison journey rendering two compared series with checkbox re-request; editor multi-value draft→Apply round trip with canonical persisted filters), `pnpm check:contracts` (OpenAPI current), `pnpm check:doc-links` (807 links) and `pnpm check:contrast` all pass.
- **Result:** Implemented as specified: one verified dimension (treasury recipient) with immutable mapping/filter schema (new `charts/filter_registry.py` re-pointed by the registry), server-side multi-selection comparison, exact-decimal full-universe reconciliation, retention of source/unit/coverage context in saved instances, insight ResolvedContext and export IR (including the markdown filters line), old chart versions still rendering unchanged, no unsupported classifications, no migration (registry/dataset versions are code + append-only DB registration). ### NI-046 — Define structured insight and evidence node contracts

- **Epic:** 10 — Insights and Evidence
- **Priority:** P0
- **Dependencies:** NI-036, NI-038
- **Complexity:** M
- **Status:** DONE
- **Claimed by:** Claude agent for role B (ILYUTKICK), session dispatch #23
- **Execution role:** B
- **Started at:** 2026-11-08T01:44:41+03:00
- **Branch/worktree:** feat/ni-046-insight-contracts; /Users/ilyutkinn/Desktop/near-intelligence-b7 (worktree cut from origin/main d25df3e)
- **Dependency verification:** NI-036 DONE (canonical EditorDocument validator, revision service and the insight/hypothesis/evidence projection tables of migration 0006 on main) and NI-038 DONE (Tiptap custom block prototype) — both verified in docs/TASKS.md on this branch before the claim.

**Implemented (2026-11-08, this claim):** `apps/api/src/near_api/insights/schemas.py` centralizes the typed research annotations shared by editor/API/projections: `InsightInput`/`Insight`, `HypothesisInput`/`Hypothesis`, `EvidenceInput`/`Evidence`, `RiskInput` (canonical risk node contract — no convenience REST route is specified for risk, so it serves editor/projections/export), frozen `ResolvedContext`/`ModelContext` and the typed rich-text node shapes. Design decisions recorded for review:

- **Node taxonomy alignment (no NI-036 extension required).** The four insight types (`observation|hypothesis|risk|conclusion`), three evidence classifications (`supporting|contradicting|uncertain`), five authored assessments (`untested|supported|mixed|weakened|insufficient_data`), four target kinds (`insight|chart_block|model_run|citation`) and both context shapes were verified identical between EDITOR_SPECIFICATION, API_SPECIFICATION and the existing `documents/schema.py` node parsers — no divergence, so the contract module DERIVES its Literal enums from `documents/schema.py`'s tuples (one authoritative schema) instead of re-declaring them, and the test matrix pins the normative values. The only `documents/schema.py` change is additive: the public `canonical_rich_body_json` reuse wrapper exposing the existing bounded/sanitized rich-text grammar (bounds, whitelist, no duplicated rule).
- **Context contents.** `ResolvedContext` carries the selected range (`selection.start`/`selection.end` on the shared `schemas.common.Selection` contract: ISO dates, half-open non-empty ≤366 days), the filters as passed (the approved filter domain is empty until a filter schema is registered), the immutable dataset pin (`dataset_version_id` UUID — chosen over dataset_key/version because the pin is an opaque immutable version reference and `context_hash` already binds the rest), plus `chart_definition_version_id`, `metric_definition_versions` (≤100), `renderer_version`, `resolved_at`. `ModelContext` carries `model_run_id`/`dataset_version_id`/`specification_version`/`context_hash`. Both forbid unknown fields; a context with neither or both discriminating keys is rejected.
- **Stable IDs: uuid4 allocation, never uuid5-of-content.** `block_id` and the optional `hypothesis_id` link are client-suppliable UUIDs per API_SPECIFICATION ("otherwise client supplies a new UUID"); entity ids (`Insight.id` etc.) are response-only. Ids are allocated once at creation and persist unchanged across revisions; deriving them from content would churn ids on every statement/body edit and break evidence links and outline anchors (NI-048 explicitly requires references to survive text edits). Uniqueness inside a document stays enforced by the canonical validator (duplicate insight/hypothesis/evidence/block ids rejected).
- **Author timestamps server-side (fixed rule).** Input contracts carry no timestamp fields; `extra="forbid"` turns client-supplied `authored_at`/`modified_at`/`created_at`/`updated_at` into a typed 422, as it does for `owner_id`/`user_id` (owner always derives from the verified principal, NI-019 `verified_owner_id` pattern — no model declares an owner field). Response contracts add `authored_at`/`modified_at` as required server-authored `ServerTimestamp` fields (ISO 8601 with explicit timezone required — epoch numbers rejected like `Observation` — normalized to UTC). Canonical editor nodes keep persisting these values verbatim across revisions (immutable history); the convenience services of NI-047/NI-048 author the values when annotations are created/mutated and PATCH preserves the original `authored_at`.
- **Rich-text bodies bounded/sanitized by reuse, not duplication.** Insight/risk bodies validate through `documents/schema.py`'s canonical parser (paragraph/list grammar, bold/italic/link marks only, depth ≤3, per-node text bounds, http(s)-only credential-free link targets, ≤10k plain-text-equivalent chars per API_SPECIFICATION); the Pydantic rich-text models are the typed projection of what that parser emits so the generated TS client gets precise node unions while the whitelist stays defined once. Statements (≤2000), explanations (≤2000), titles (≤200) reuse the schema constants directly.
- **TS generation without empty routes.** `INSIGHT_CONTRACT_MODELS` is registered in the OpenAPI `components` through the existing NI-004 `contracts.py` mechanism (`models_json_schema`, conflict-checked) — no fabricated routes; the convenience endpoints arrive with NI-047/NI-048/NI-049 and reuse these models. `packages/shared-types/openapi.json` + `src/generated/api.ts` regenerated and committed.
- **No migration.** The projections zone of migration 0006 already covers the annotations: `app.insights` (JSONB `body`/`context` + server timestamps + typed columns), `app.hypotheses`, `app.evidence_links` (CHECK constraints for classification/target_kind/assessment) — verified against `db/models.py`; nothing to add, so no 0010 migration and no change to projections or `run-db-tests.py` discovery. `RiskInput` needs no table: risk nodes live in canonical content/blocks attrs per the NI-036 design.

- **Verification (2026-11-08, this claim):** 1) `uv run --project apps/api python -m unittest discover -s apps/api/tests -p "test_*.py" -v` — 401 tests OK (skipped=123, exactly the cluster-dependent cases), including the new offline `test_insights.py` suite (19 tests: four insight types × three classifications, five assessments, four target kinds, unsupported-value/unknown-field/owner-field/timestamp rejections, context required-field and bounds matrix incl. 367-day window and epoch/naive timestamps, stable-ID validation and normalization, rich-text sanitize/bounds matrix with html/iframe/javascript:/credentials/depth-4/10k-over cases, canonical JSON round-trip preserving `content_sha256`, response models mapped from canonical nodes, OpenAPI component publication without new routes). 2) `uv run --project apps/api python scripts/run-db-tests.py --pg-bin /opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@18/bin` — PostgreSQL 18 disposable loopback cluster: all 15 discovered suites OK (293 integration tests incl. test_documents 30, test_delivery_apis 28, test_ownership_rls 17), no skips, exit 0. 3) `uv lock --check --project apps/api` — OK, no dependency changes (no new Python dependencies). 4) `pnpm check:contracts` — OpenAPI and TypeScript current; `pnpm --filter @near-intelligence/shared-types typecheck` and `test` pass (generated client compiles with the new contract types). 5) `pnpm check:doc-links` — 587 links resolve. 6) `git diff --check` — clean.

Each group shows the statement, the authored assessment kept separate from the counts, the evidence entries (classification label, target-kind label, short target id, explanation) with contradicting entries visually framed and surfaced under a "Counterarguments" heading, "Related risks" (risk blocks with `related_hypothesis_id`), and explicit empty states at every level (no hypotheses, no linked evidence, no counterarguments, no risks, load failure) — classification is labelled editorial throughout, never a confidence claim. Navigation is strictly read-only: no attribute/insight-context rewrite, no dirtying (the save status stays clean, verified in tests). Panel state notes: "Linked evidence from saved revision N", with an honest unsaved-edits note; refetches on acknowledged-revision change and on explicit Retry; loading/error/stale states; ARIA labels per entry and per group; entries are real buttons (Enter/Space) with the shared visible-focus outline. `editor-shell.tsx` mounts the panel under Properties in the desktop tools pane and the tools drawer and wires the polite announcement channel. **Approval mechanics decision (content-addressed operator approval file):** publication is an operator CLI flow (API_SPECIFICATION: "Creating datasets/models/publications uses the operator CLI, not a browser POST API"), so review is recorded as `publication_approval.json` next to the reviewed artifacts: `decision: "approve"`, non-empty `reviewer` and `review_reference` (the review that approved these artifacts) and `approved_artifacts` — the SHA-256 of each reviewed manifest over the canonical rendering shared with the research CLIs. `research verify --offline` prints those hashes so the operator can copy them into the approval; the gate recomputes every hash and refuses publication when the reviewed bytes and the published bytes diverge, so an approval can never be silently re-bound to different artifacts. The approval document is stored verbatim on the published row (its own review provenance) and travels in the served bundle. A stronger signature scheme is not assumed available (REPRODUCIBILITY: "Signed/provider-authenticated receipts are not assumed available").

**Migration decision (0010_model_runs, recorded delta to DATABASE_SCHEMA.md):** the implemented `app.model_runs` is one write-once published-only table instead of the pre-implementation status-lifecycle row: a run exists in the database ONLY after the gate verified the complete bundle, so `rejected_readiness/running/failed/succeeded` have no storage and can never look like results (the estimator already refuses unadmitted specs; the gate verdict is recorded on the row it publishes). `run_id` is content-derived (UUIDv5 over the three manifest hashes) making re-publication idempotent and a re-approval of the same content under a different approval a refused conflict; denormalized identity columns (spec identity, verdict, code commit, lock hash, tolerances, window/nobs) are re-asserted by CHECK constraints against the stored `manifests` JSONB so a row can never disagree with itself; FORCE RLS with `near_operator` as the only writer and read-only `near_app` (0005 pattern); no UPDATE/DELETE grant for any role. The `model_definition_version_id`/`dataset_version_id` FKs of the pre-implementation row stay unwritten because published runs bind the offline archive manifests recorded in their data manifests (not a database dataset version) — a dataset-bound run identity is a contract change for a future task, and the readiness endpoint's `dataset_version` parameter is therefore not implemented (recorded in API_SPECIFICATION).

### NI-056 — Create frozen document export representation

- **Epic:** 12 — Research Export
- **Priority:** P0
- **Dependencies:** NI-043, NI-050, NI-054
- **Complexity:** M
- **Status:** DONE
- **Claimed by:** Claude role-B agent (ILYUTKICK operator dispatch, ZCode session), NI-056 dispatch
- **Execution role:** B
- **Started at:** 2026-11-09T19:59:49+03:00
- **Branch/worktree:** feat/ni-056-export-ir; /Users/ilyutkinn/Desktop/near-intelligence-b10 (worktree stacked on feat/ni-050-evidence-navigation head 0eaacfb)
- **Dependency verification:** NI-043 DONE on origin/main (merged); NI-054 DONE on origin/main (merged PR #57). NI-050 is implemented, independently role-B-reviewed (approved, PR comment) and CI-green on OPEN PR #76 awaiting owner merge — claim basis recorded per the NI-037/NI-036 open-PR dependency precedent; this PR targets the NI-050 branch and must be retargeted to main immediately after #76 merges, before the owner merges it.

**Description:** Translate an acknowledged immutable revision into a complete export plan.

**Implementation steps:** Read owned exact version; validate all pins/citations; map every P0 block into deterministic intermediate representation; include title/author/date/source/model references and data-table specs.

**Expected files/modules:** apps/api/src/near_api/exports/ir.py; schemas/export.py

**Acceptance criteria:** No latest-data lookups; all required references resolvable; unsupported/missing block fails with detail; title/content correspond to same revision.

**Testing requirements:** Mutate current document/source after request and compare frozen IR; every block mapper; missing artifact and cross-owner cases.

- **Result:** `apps/api/src/near_api/exports/` (`__init__.py`, `ir.py`) + `apps/api/src/near_api/schemas/export.py` — the frozen export IR of one acknowledged immutable revision. The typed `ExportIr` contract (service-level Pydantic, `extra="forbid"`, frozen) carries `ir_version: 1`, revision metadata (document/version id, revision, schema version, content hash, title, author = verified owner + profile display name, the revision's immutable `created_at`), a discriminated per-block mapping covering EVERY canonical P0 type (paragraph runs with bold/italic/link marks, heading level+runs, bullet/ordered list items, liveChart with the fully resolved frozen `ChartViewResponse` (NI-032 contract reused verbatim — series points as exact decimal strings with quality/bounds, pinned `resolved_at`, sources; snapshot mode can never reach the plan because NI-043 reconstruction refuses it), dataTable with column keys/labels/types/units and either authored cells exported as exact decimal strings (int directly, finite float via shortest round-trip Decimal in plain notation — `1e30` exports as the full integer string, never binary rounding) or the approved dataset pin (dataset_key/version/content_sha256), insight/hypothesis cards with pinned contexts and authored timestamps, evidence with `target_block_id` for same-revision insight/chart targets, risk cards, dividers) and the deduplicated deterministic `sources`/`model_runs`/`citations` reference registries. Frozen semantics: chart evidence resolves ONLY through `documents.context_resolver.reconstruct_revision_chart` (NI-043 — revision/context hash, chart/metric/renderer version and pinned prepared-data digest verification reused, nothing duplicated); insight chart contexts re-verify through the shared `verify_chart_context` pin chain; dataTable dataset pins re-verify approved through the shared `require_approved_dataset`; evidence `model_run`/`citation` targets re-verify through NI-049's `validate_public_targets` plus the same published-only catalog for insight `ModelContext` runs (the context's `dataset_version_id` is recorded verbatim — per the recorded NI-054 delta, published runs bind offline archive manifests, not a database dataset version, so that pin is not database-resolvable and is deliberately not failed on). Everything else fails closed with typed `ExportIrError(message, details)` (bounded (path, reason) details): missing/inconsistent pinned artifact (`pinned_artifact_unavailable`, `pinned_definition_unavailable`, `chart_context_mismatch`, `missing_chart_context` details carried from the reconstruction error), unapproved/absent dataset reference, dangling public target, invalid stored content (canonical parse errors carried with their detail paths), invalid rich text/cells, contract drift (`unsupported_block_type` — the mapper dispatch is exhaustive over the canonical taxonomy and a missing entry is a typed failure, never a silent skip), and `revision_not_acknowledged` for a valid but superseded version. Title/content correspondence is structural: the plan is built only while the requested version is the document's `current_version_id` read in the same transaction — every title change creates a new revision (NI-037 rename semantics), so the exported title and content always describe the same revision; a revision that stopped being acknowledged is never re-exported with the current title (typed refusal, honest limit — historical revisions carry no title snapshot). Determinism: no wall-clock field is added (`export_ir_canonical_bytes` = sorted-key compact UTF-8 JSON with `allow_nan=False`); building twice for the same revision yields byte-identical output. Ownership: `get_document`/`get_version` compositional NI-019 checks inside one `principal_transaction`; absent and not-owned ids are the one 404-equivalent. No new Python dependency; no migration; no OpenAPI/client change (see handoff note).
- **Handoff (role A / NI-057+NI-058):** (1) No FastAPI route is in this card's scope and none was added: NI-058 owns POST `/documents/{id}/exports`, the export-job migrations and the download surface; when a route first consumes `ExportIr`, the models must be registered in the OpenAPI components through `contracts.py` and the generated client regenerated (packages/**) — deliberately NOT done here to avoid a silent contract change (the card's instruction not to add a route silently is honored by this note). (2) The acknowledged-revision rule is the recorded contract decision: the export job must build/hold the IR while the pinned version is still the document's current revision (the API_SPECIFICATION "client first saves then uses acknowledged version ID" flow), or NI-058 must add a per-job title/content pin via its own migrations if deferred worker processing after renames must keep exporting a superseded revision — per-revision titles are not stored anywhere today, so this builder refuses rather than fabricate attribution. (3) NI-057 renders static chart assets from the IR's embedded per-chart `view` payloads (chart data/options/quality already equal the frozen view) and NI-058 renders Markdown from the block IR + source/model/citation registries; `export_ir_canonical_bytes` is the plan's deterministic storage/comparison format. (4) Evidence citation targets export as immutable public artifact ids (the artifact registry has no public title — NI-049 precedent); labels are renderer scope.
- **Verification:** 1) Focused new suite `uv run --project apps/api python -m unittest discover -s apps/api/tests -p "test_exports_ir.py"` — 18 tests OK (6 offline + 12 cluster-gated skips offline). 2) Full offline discovery `uv run --project apps/api python -m unittest discover -s apps/api/tests -p "test_*.py"` — 539 tests OK (skipped=194: exactly the cluster/real-archive-gated cases; the new suite contributes 6 run + 12 gated skips, no new skipped state beyond its own gated class). 3) Disposable-cluster suite `uv run --project apps/api python scripts/run-db-tests.py --pg-bin /opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@18/bin` — exit 0, all 21 discovered suites (now including `test_exports_ir.py`) OK: 426 integration tests, 0 skips (suite summaries 8/4/9/13/9/16/39/35/26/31/6/10/29/22/14/17/20/30/53/17/18). The new DB class covers: every P0 block mapper against a real saved document with a registered chart + published synthetic dataset (frozen view values, pinned resolved_at, decimal-string cells, dataset identity, targets), double-build byte identity, frozen IR after rename+new revision (held plan byte-identical; superseded rebuild refused; fresh plan from the new acknowledged revision), frozen IR after a newer dataset publication with `delivery.resolve_latest_version` monkeypatched to raise (structural no-latest proof) and rebuilt byte-identical, missing pinned artifact (manifest digest corrupted via admin — typed `pinned_artifact_unavailable` with block path), unapproved dataTable dataset pin (typed `dangling_dataset_reference`), dangling public model-run/citation targets on injected revisions (typed details), a published `ModelContext` insight (injected revision) exporting the run reference with spec identity, an unverifiable insight context (typed failure), invalid stored content (typed canonical parse detail), Alice/Bob denial including version-UUID-without-document access, and the non-acknowledged revision refusal. The published model run comes from the NI-054 synthetic bundle (idempotent content-derived `run_id`, order-independent with the NI-054 suites). 4) `uv lock --check --project apps/api` — OK (no dependency change; `apps/api/uv.lock` untouched). 5) Ruff: no ruff configuration exists in this repository (nothing in pyproject or CI; the CONTRIBUTING.md line `uv run --project apps/api ruff check .` does not resolve because ruff is not in the frozen dependency set and this task may not change the lock) — run as a best-effort signal with pinned standalone `uvx ruff@0.14.6 check` (default rules) over `apps/api/src/near_api/exports/`, `apps/api/src/near_api/schemas/export.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_exports_ir.py`, `scripts/run-db-tests.py`: all checks passed (the pre-existing tree has 73 findings outside this task's scope, unchanged by it). 6) `node scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` — 641 links resolve. 7) Three iterative cluster failures during development were fixed and re-run to green (RichTextBody TypeAdapter, RichTextList content field, SQLAlchemy 2.1 entity-select semantics on the published-run lookup, admin-role artifact UPDATE) — the recorded numbers are from the final clean run. Desktop/mobile passes not required (desktop-only scope, 8 Nov 2026).

### NI-057 — Render static chart assets from frozen contexts

- **Epic:** 12 — Research Export
- **Priority:** P0
- **Dependencies:** NI-056, NI-033
- **Complexity:** M
- **Status:** DONE
- **Claimed by:** Claude role-A coordinator (ZCode session, ILYUTKICK operator), NI-057 dispatch — one subagent covers the A Node-renderer zone and the B Python-subprocess zone per the 9 November 2026 parallel-dispatch/no-review instruction
- **Execution role:** A + B in a single dispatch
- **Started at:** 2026-11-09T21:47:37+03:00
- **Branch/worktree:** feat/ni-057-chart-assets; /Users/ilyutkinn/Desktop/near-intelligence-a15 (worktree from origin/main 916ac87)
- **Dependency verification:** NI-056 DONE on main (merged PR #77, 916ac87; card closure rides the docs commit in PR #78); NI-033 DONE on main (merged PR #49). No competing NI-057 claim and no open PR conflict at session start.

**Description:** Reuse chart data/options for portable exported evidence.

**Implementation steps:** Add pinned Node ECharts SSR renderer with typed stdin schema; Python subprocess without shell/network; generate SVG plus normalized CSV/hash; enforce timeout/size and safe filenames.

**Expected files/modules:** apps/web/scripts/render-charts.mjs; apps/api/src/near_api/exports/charts.py

**Acceptance criteria:** Export series/units/quality equal frozen view; no arbitrary remote/HTML code; renderer version/hash recorded; corrupt input fails safely.

**Testing requirements:** Compare chart CSV to pinned series; subprocess timeout and injection/path traversal; SVG safe output and no-network test.

- **Result:** `apps/web/scripts/render-charts.mjs` — the pinned Node ECharts SSR renderer. Strict zod stdin envelope (`protocol_version` 1, strict keys, canonical-lowercase-UUID chart ids, bounded width/height/charts≤200/series≤32/points≤2000; no URL/HTML/code field exists, unknown envelope keys are rejected outright and unknown view fields are stripped — a render-boundary mirror in the established `catalog-schema.ts` convention, while the render types still come ONLY from the generated shared-types through the reused NI-033 builder). Each chart renders from the IR's FROZEN embedded `ChartViewResponse` via `buildChart(view, { interactions: false, animation: false })` — imported as TypeScript through Node 24 erasable-syntax type stripping plus a narrow extensionless-to-`.ts` resolution hook, so there is still exactly one rendering implementation (no second renderer); the echarts 6.1.0 imports stay modular per ADR-019 and register the same component set as `chart-factory.ts`. Output per chart: a deterministic SVG (zrender's globally counter-derived `zrN-cls-M` style classes and `zrN-cK` clip-path ids are rewritten to canonical first-appearance aliases inside `class`/`id`/`url(#…)`/`href="#…"` and `<style>` selectors — nothing else is touched; byte-identity proven across separate runs AND within one multi-chart request) plus a lossless long-format CSV (`date,series_index,series,unit,value,value_kind,quality,quality_reasons`; exact decimal strings verbatim with RFC4180 quoting, missing points export as empty values with their recorded reason — never zero) and per-asset SHA-256; the response carries the renderer identity (`near-export-chart-renderer@1`), echarts 6.1.0, node version and the SHA-256 of the exact request bytes. Every SVG must pass static-safety assertions (no `<script`/`<foreignObject`/`<image`, no `javascript:`, no remote `href`/`url(…)`, no `data:text/html`, no event-handler attributes) or the renderer fails closed; non-conforming input exits 2/3 with a typed `{error:{code,message,details}}` (`invalid_json`, `invalid_payload`, `invalid_input_size`, `unsupported_renderer`, `asset_size_exceeded`). `apps/api/src/near_api/exports/charts.py` — the Python boundary: typed service contracts (wire `ChartRendererResponse` + `ChartRenderResult` with the boundary-computed `script_sha256`; `chart_render_request()` builds the request from the NI-056 `ExportChartBlock`s verbatim — no dataset/definition/latest resolution exists on this path); `subprocess.run` with a fixed ARGV list (`node --no-warnings <script>` — no shell anywhere, payload on STDIN as strict JSON bytes, timeout kills the child → `renderer_timeout`, missing node/script → honest `renderer_unavailable`); size caps (4 MiB request, 64 MiB stdout, 12 MiB per asset); wire verification (renderer identity, request-hash equality, chart-set equality in request order, per-asset hash recomputation, series/point coverage against the pinned view); every SVG re-scanned with the same safety patterns (a controlled fake renderer emitting an event-handler attribute is refused by the Python side even though it bypassed the Node scan); `safe_chart_asset_name()` derives filenames from canonical lowercase UUID block ids only — traversal, separators, shell metacharacters, control characters, uppercase/braced UUIDs and bad extensions are typed refusals, never sanitized guesses; `ChartRendererError(code, details)` is raised for every failure mode and never substituted with live data. `NEAR_CHART_RENDERER_NODE`/`NEAR_CHART_RENDERER_SCRIPT` are operator/testing environment seams, never caller input, and no raw filesystem path is accepted from a request. Handoff to NI-058: embed `ChartRenderResult` assets + identity in the ZIP manifest; no route, no OpenAPI/client change, no new Python or npm dependency, no migration. Limitations: ECharts SSR does not embed the aria description in the SVG — the accessible alternative of an exported chart is its CSV plus the NI-058 Markdown (the image itself carries no text alternative beyond the summary-free frame); the recorded identity includes the node patch version, so identical SVG/CSV bytes ship with an environment-specific identity line; pixel identity across platforms is explicitly not guaranteed (CHART_SYSTEM); the type-stripping reuse requires node ≥23.6 (engines pin node 24).
- **Verification:** 1) Focused Node suite `pnpm --filter web exec vitest run tests/exports/render-charts.test.ts` — 10/10 (CSV byte-equality with the pinned series incl. a 31-significant-digit decimal, a gap row with reason and a lower bound; cross-run and within-request byte-identity; sha256 record incl. raw request hash; static-safety of the SVG; corrupt stdin; six schema-violation classes with bounded typed details; oversized input before parsing; foreign-renderer refusal; offline source scan). 2) Focused Python suite `uv run --project apps/api python -m unittest discover -s apps/api/tests -p "test_exports_charts.py"` — 22 tests OK (contract shapes and strict JSON payload with exact decimal strings; safe-name matrix of 21 hostile ids and 8 bad extensions; invocation seam asserting an ARGV list, no `shell` kwarg and STDIN bytes; missing node binary and missing script as typed honest errors; patched and real controlled-slow-renderer timeout with the child actually killed <4s; corrupt/refusing fakes; identity, input-hash, asset-hash and chart-set tampering refusals; unsafe-SVG refusal; oversized output; the real pinned renderer producing assets equal to the frozen series, deterministic). 3) Full offline discovery `uv run --project apps/api python -m unittest discover -s apps/api/tests -p "test_*.py"` — 561 tests OK (skipped=194: exactly the documented cluster/real-archive gated classes; **Description:** Provide one reliable complete MVP export rather than several incomplete formats.

**Implementation steps:** Add export-job/idempotency/audit migrations as needed; implement owned queued export jobs/lease/retry/TTL; generate research.md/assets/CSV/manifest ZIP; status polling/download; exclude private filesystem paths and sanitized secret-bearing metadata.

**Expected files/modules:** apps/api/src/near_api/exports/; export worker CLI; editor Export dialog

**Acceptance criteria:** User downloads exact revision with charts/notes/sources/model refs/timestamps; Bob cannot poll/download; expired artifacts return typed state; late edits do not affect bundle.

**Testing requirements:** Unzip full research export and validate hashes/links; worker crash/retry idempotency; owner matrix; edit-during-export and expiry.

- **Result:** The full Markdown ZIP export pipeline. (1) Migration `0012_export_jobs` — `app.export_jobs` with the frozen plan (bytea, 32 MiB cap) and the finished ZIP (bytea + sha256 + size) stored INSIDE the RLS-protected row (recorded delta vs the pre-implementation `artifact_id` column: the scientific `artifacts` table deliberately keeps `near_app` read-only per NI-028, so owner-written export bytes live in the owner-scoped job row — no public artifact key, no server filesystem path, no signed private URL); same-owner composite FKs to documents/document_versions with ON DELETE CASCADE (the NI-037-recorded export-job cascade); `UNIQUE(owner_id, idempotency_key)`; lifecycle CHECKs (status enum `queued/running/succeeded/failed/expired`, bundle/hash/size pairing, typed `error_code` only on `failed`, lease pairing, attempt accounting, terminal `completed_at`); FORCE RLS — `near_app` self policies over every column, `near_operator` narrow lease power over lifecycle columns only (never plan/bundle bytes) plus the operator-cascade DELETE purge; mirrored in `db/models.py` for the drift check. (2) `exports/service.py` — idempotent create (advisory lock over owner/document/key, replay from the job row itself so a post-retention key reuse still answers with the same job's honest state, same-key different body 409), the plan built in the SAME transaction via NI-056's `build_export_ir_in_transaction` (superseded revision → typed 409 `REVISION_NOT_ACKNOWLEDGED`; unresolvable pin/reference → 422 `REFERENCE_INVALID`; oversized plan → 422 `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE`), owner-checked poll with READ-TIME expiry (a past-TTL artifact reports `expired` before any sweep) and the download path re-verifying the stored bytes against their SHA-256 (`EXPORT_CORRUPT` otherwise). (3) `exports/routes.py` — POST `/api/documents/{document_id}/exports` (202, `Idempotency-Key` honored, PDF refused as typed 422 `FEATURE_UNAVAILABLE`), GET `.../exports/{job_id}`, GET `.../exports/{job_id}/download` (document-nested so absent/not-owned/cross-document ids are one generic 404 — Bob cannot create, poll or download; no-store everywhere; Content-Disposition filename is server-controlled `near-export-{job_id}.zip`). (4) `exports/markdown.py` — deterministic inert-text Markdown renderer of the plan: headings/paragraph runs with bold/italic/link marks (structure characters escaped, newlines flattened — no Markdown/HTML injection), lists, chart embeds with relative asset links + pinned captions, exact decimal-string data tables (authored rows and dataset-pin notes), insight/hypothesis/evidence/risk cards with pinned contexts, divider, source/model-run/citation registries and a Limitations section carrying the recorded absence reasons and bound kinds; exhaustive block dispatch with a typed `MarkdownRenderError` drift guard. (5) `exports/bundle.py` — bundle assembly + `manifest.json` (`bundle_format_version` 1, IR version, revision metadata, `plan_sha256` over `export_ir_canonical_bytes`, per-file sha256/byte sizes, NI-057 renderer identity record, per-chart asset records, the plan's limitations, fixed `zip_entry_timestamp` — no wall-clock field) + fixed-timestamp/sorted-entry/create-system ZIP packaging + the acceptance verifiers (`extract_bundle` refusing hostile members, `verify_bundle` hash/size validation, `verify_research_links`). (6) `exports/worker.py` — the operator CLI `python -m near_api.exports.worker` (`--database-url` near_app credential for per-job OWNER transactions, `--operator-database-url` for the lease; `--sweep`): claim = one operator transaction `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` over queued/lease-expired rows with a fresh lease and attempts+1 and the two-attempt cap turning zombies into typed `failed lease_attempts_exhausted`; load = plan bytes read as the job's owner through RLS and verified against `plan_sha256` before rendering; render = NI-057 subprocess boundary + bundle builder (typed failure codes on the job, never a partial artifact); commit = lease-guarded owner UPDATE (a lost lease drops the bytes and the retry regenerates them from the same frozen plan — idempotent by construction); sweep = per-owner expiration + byte clearing. No shell anywhere; no secret/DSN/path ever written to a row or the manifest. (7) Role-A zone: regenerated generated client (`createDocumentExport`/`getDocumentExport`/`downloadDocumentExport` + `DocumentExportRequest`/`ExportJobResponse` via the generator, committed), `apps/web/src/features/editor/exports/export-api.ts` (private-fetch client, typed `ExportApiError` from the shared error envelope, client UUID idempotency key, no owner identifier anywhere) and `export-dialog.tsx` (create → bounded 1s×120 poll → automatic download through the shared blob helper; typed failed-with-error-code, expired, slow-poll, 404/401 states; retry creates a NEW job with a fresh key; Radix dialog with Escape/close, focus return, polite announcements via the editor live region; ready state never claims a download that did not happen) wired into the editor header behind the same clean/draft gate as revision history, with `current_version_id` carried through the workbench. Registered in main.py; documented in API_SPECIFICATION (route table updated to the implemented document-nested paths — the polling/download paths are document-nested rather than the pre-implementation top-level `/exports/{jobId}`, recorded as the implemented delta), DATABASE_SCHEMA (implemented-shape note), REPRODUCIBILITY and apps/api/README. Honest limitations: the worker is operator-invoked CLI (no daemon/scheduler in MVP — a queued job processes when an operator runs the worker); render-failure retry is a NEW job (a failed job stays failed for the record); a user reusing an idempotency key after TTL replays the expired job (honest state, new key for a fresh export); the API_SPECIFICATION rate-limit targets (exports 3/min/user, one active job/user) are policy targets and are not enforced in code; the manifest cannot carry its own hash (it pins every other member); ZIP deflate bytes are identical per-environment (fixed timestamps/order) but not promised identical across zlib releases — verification is hash-validated extraction.

The uv resolution cutoff was NOT bumped; `uv lock` resolved inside it, the lock diff was reviewed, `uv lock --check` passes, root `requirements.txt` regenerated from the frozen export. (2) `exports/pdf.py` — pure function `render_pdf(plan, assets)` mirroring `render_markdown`'s contract: attribution block (title, author, revision, content hash, recorded creation time, IR version + plan SHA-256, PDF layout version 1), the frozen-revision statement WITH the Markdown-bundle `manifest.json` reference, headings/paragraph runs (bold/italic/link)/bullet+ordered lists with nested indentation, insight/hypothesis/evidence/risk cards with the pinned-context lines (`_context_lines` semantics), an exhaustive block dispatch that fails closed typed (`unsupported_block_type`) — including the NI-055 `modelResult` branch implemented duck-typed against that block's recorded shape (summary pins, verbatim coefficient table, interpretation, diagnostics, manifest) so the combined tree renders it while this base's grammar cannot produce it, every chart as the NI-057 SSR SVG embedded as a VECTOR drawing via svglib (font-family rewritten to the registered DejaVu faces; scaled to printable width with aspect preserved and a page-height cap — an unembeddable/degenerate asset is a typed failure `chart_embed_failed`, never a crop; the SVG was already static-safety-scanned at the NI-057 boundary, so no screenshot/fetch path exists anywhere), a caption per figure carrying the chart-definition/dataset pins + resolved-at + appendix table reference, a per-figure exact-value appendix table rendering the CSV asset verbatim (RFC-4180 parse; ragged row = typed refusal; exact decimal strings never reformatted; missing values stay empty with their recorded quality reason; bounds carry their kinds), the Sources/Model runs/Citations registries, the plan's Limitations, and a rendering-provenance record (renderer identity, request/script SHA-256) plus the no-live-data/no-screenshot statement. ### NI-060 — Implement revocable read-only sharing

- **Epic:** 12 — Research Export
- **Priority:** P2
- **Dependencies:** NI-058, NI-062, NI-043
- **Complexity:** L
- **Status:** DONE
- **Claimed by:** ZCode implementation subagent, dispatched by coordinator session sess_9345ee4f-f534-4698-98d2-5809986ac4c3
- **Execution role:** B with A sharing UI handoff (implemented in-agent)
- **Started at:** 2026-11-12T16:52:42Z
- **Branch/worktree:** feat/ni-060-sharing; /Users/ilyutkinn/Desktop/near-intelligence-a29
- **Authorization:** Owner handoff v2 (docs/FINISH_BEFORE_DEPLOY_PROMPT.md, 12 November 2026) supersedes the former design-only card and requires a working feature; PR #88 carries the updated registry.
- **Dependency verification:** NI-058, NI-062 and NI-043 are DONE in this registry; verified on docs/ni-077-handoff-v2 at commit 2787fd5.
- **Scope update:** Owner 2026-11-12 requires a working feature before deployment; the former design-only card is superseded, not considered implementation evidence.

**Description:** Implement explicit, revocable read-only publication of a chosen frozen research revision, separate from private drafts and private UUID routes.

**Implementation steps:** Define bounded consent/snapshot/revocation/expiry design and threat model; update API/schema/ADR before code. Implement owner-managed create/list/revoke with high-entropy tokens stored only as hashes; immutable allowlisted publication projection, scoped assets and read-only desktop viewer. Preview exactly what will be exposed before explicit publication. Propagate revocation/expiry to every asset/download/cache path; exclude private editorial metadata and restricted source bytes. Local QA publishes only synthetic disposable content; actual external sharing needs user consent.

**Expected files/modules:** sharing ADR; API publication/schema/migrations; generated contracts; web sharing dialog/viewer; frozen publication assets and security tests.

**Acceptance criteria:** Real local create→read as non-owner→revoke/expire→deny works for the same frozen revision and all assets. A share grants no private draft/history/list/search/write/export access; edits and dataset refresh never mutate the publication. No token/secret leaks to logs, referrers or unsafe caches; unsupported/restricted content fails explicitly. Consent names revision and visible scope. A design document or disabled button cannot close this card.

**Testing requirements:** Owner/Alice/Bob/anonymous matrix, token guessing/invalid/expiry/revocation and asset access, frozen content/hash invariance, local browser consent/view/revoke flow, cache/header/secret checks and threat-model verification. No prerequisite on deployment or NI-079.

### NI-061 — Add business/data integration regression suite

- **Epic:** 13 — Quality and Security
- **Priority:** P0
- **Dependencies:** NI-035, NI-050

**Description:** Verify financial and research invariants across DB/API/editor boundaries.

**Implementation steps:** Create independent event oracles; cover exact sums/internal hops/failed receipts/missing data/bounds; test revision projections and prepared-dataset membership; isolate all fixtures from production.

**Expected files/modules:** apps/api/tests/integration/; apps/web tests/; CI test data

**Acceptance criteria:** Invariant suite catches double counting and source mutation; fixture labels explicit; no tests alter archives or access paid providers.

**Testing requirements:** Run focused suite against real PostgreSQL; exact monetary assertions; document graph and API-contract regressions.

- **Result:** `apps/api/tests/integration/` (new package, recursive-discovery covered offline and cluster): `oracles.py` — the independent oracle, deliberately different code from `near_api.metrics` (plain-integer yoctoNEAR arithmetic and straight-line matching; only the power-of-ten yocto→NEAR division uses Decimal): per-day/total purchase sums and event counts with duplicate collapse per canonical identity, payout matching on receiver+exact amount within 300 s with single-use receipts and zero-or-multiple candidates refused (`OraclePayoutError`), payout bucketed on the matched transfer's UTC cash-receipt day, internal-hop/other-sender/failed/out-of-window exclusion and reporting, and supply stock/window net-change recomputation from the saved summary; `invariant_fixtures.py` — one synthetic raw saved-extract bundle in the real `{"kind","payload"}` shape (files and quality notes visibly labeled "synthetic"): matched payout pairs including a 23:57-withdrawal whose receipt lands 00:01 next UTC date (240 s, both sides of midnight), an exact-300 s match, verbatim duplicate conversion+transfer rows, internal treasury hop, other-sender inflow, failed receipt, out-of-window rows, a one-day price gap (with 3.25/1.5 prices chosen so USD products stay exactly representable), provider series, pinned boundary stocks with conservative bounds and a treasury snapshot; `test_invariants_metrics.py` (17 offline tests) — oracle vs `aggregate_purchases`/`match_payouts` exact per-day/total agreement, exclusion/duplicate agreement (every in-window successful treasury transfer is exactly matched payout + reported hop + reported other-sender), zero/ambiguous/single-use payout failures refused in BOTH implementations, emitted-manifest reconciliation (purchases/conversions/payouts per day, missing-price day value None + "missing, not zero" reason, USD 6.5/6.2 exact on priced days, gated OQ-01/OQ-08/OQ-11 nulls, supply stocks and net changes vs oracle, issuance/burn `lower` + purchases-to-issuance `upper` bound kinds with direction text and "never deducted" purchase policy, window-summary quality aggregates vs oracle, source-qualified provider series never summed), and an INCLUSIVE `app.openapi()` path pin (expected route set must be present; additions never break it; delivery families stay read-only); `test_invariants_db_chain.py` (12 real-PostgreSQL tests via `scripts/run-db-tests.py`, new discovery entry under the union rule) — staged-row exact sums and dataset-membership contract (observations ⊆ `dataset_metrics` members, stored `content_sha256` = staged digest, duplicated events counted exactly once in storage), delivery `/api/metrics/{id}/series` and `/api/charts/observed-conversions/view` equality with the oracle (Decimal equality re-scaled to yocto integers), explicit missing/unavailable delivery states (unpriced day NULL+reason in storage; multi-variant series surface explicit unavailable points, never a silent average), gated metrics delivered as nulls with OQ reasons, bounds through the delivery chain (delivered stocks = pinned boundary stocks exactly; purchases delivered separately; nothing carries supply net of purchases), frozen revision context (NI-043): `reconstruct_revision_chart` matches the direct pinned view exactly, publishing v2 moves the active pointer without changing the pinned reconstruction (context hash and all values identical), and a directly mutated approved observation row fails the reconstruction CLOSED (`ReconstructionError`, never changed values) with restore re-verified; revision projections (NI-036): canonical content re-parses to the recorded sha256 and block/insight/hypothesis/evidence rows match content exactly (ids, types, positions, target resolution within the same revision), dangling evidence target refused with zero rows, stale CAS carries the current revision with zero rows, invalid content (unknown node type) zero rows, Bob's saves and version reads are the generic 404 with zero rows; document graph and cleanup (NI-049): historical revision keeps its original evidence link after the node is removed in the next revision, Bob denied, owner delete cascades all private children to zero while `dataset_versions`/`artifacts` counts are unchanged. `apps/web/tests/editor/invariants.test.tsx` (3 vitest/jsdom tests, real workbench + NI-039 insert menu): a prose draft save carries committed liveChart and dataTable attrs VERBATIM through the real serializer (deep-equal pins; prose-only save submits no inspection claim), the If-Match chain survives a real autosave ack (next save carries `"8"` from the ETag), and a 30-significant-digit authored cell renders raw in the workbench table and serializes as a JSON string (`"…543210"` quoted; no Number() coercion on the editor boundary). No workflows file was touched: the existing recursive offline discovery and the one added runner pattern cover CI's jobs. Limitations: the oracle intentionally mirrors the DOCUMENTED matching semantics rather than duplicating the production Decimal path, so a shared semantic misreading of the docs would not be caught by it; ratio metrics (fee bps, capture-rate style ratios at the NUMERIC(78,30) scale) are asserted structurally (bound kinds, direction, no cross-source sums) instead of re-implemented, because an exact ratio oracle would duplicate the quantization formula the strategy warns against; the suite pins the delivered chain for the two emitted datasets plus the observed-conversions chart only (treasury-recipient/price-basis filter dimensions cannot match emitted `{}`/`start|end` dimension values, so filtered chart views are honestly unavailable — recorded, not exercised, to avoid asserting an ambiguity workaround); the web invariants deliberately do not re-test coordinator timing, renderer option building or the conflict dialog (owned by NI-040/NI-033/NI-042 suites); ruff is still not wired (DEPENDENCIES.md defers the first dev dependency), so the Python style gate remains absent and is reported below as not run.

Desktop/mobile passes not required (desktop-only scope, 8 Nov 2026). No test touched the research archives, collectors, providers or the network; all fixtures are synthetic and labeled.

**Known regression input from NI-041 delivery (8 November 2026):** `apps/web/scripts/check-client-secrets.mjs` includes low-entropy JWT audience/issuer/JWKS/origin metadata in its secret list. With the local audience `authenticated`, the ordinary UI state literal produces exit 1 although independent scanning finds configured issuer/JWKS/origin/signing values absent from the production client. NI-062 must correct the scanner with a positive secret-leak regression and a public-metadata false-positive regression; the NI-041 delivery did not weaken it or claim it passed.

NI-062 must reproduce and close cross-tab account-switch/logout retention for the full workbench, including pending saves, local draft recovery/download and copy-as-new behavior, and verify Alice content is never exposed or saved under Bob. This code-level gap is recorded for the required adversarial audit; NI-042 does not claim the whole editor identity boundary passed.

- **Result:** the adversarial audit is complete; the full report is [docs/qa/ni062-security-review.md](qa/ni062-security-review.md) (per-family verdicts, findings with severity, scope statement). **Zero critical findings; no unauthorized read/write/download exists in the implemented product; the release-blocking condition (any unresolved critical/high) is NOT met.** New suites: `apps/api/tests/security/` (five files: HTTP ownership matrix 7 cluster tests, direct RLS probes 9 cluster tests, session/JWT boundary 4 offline, static subprocess/network/secret sinks 9 offline, untrusted content 16 offline) and `apps/web/tests/security/` (three files, 28 tests: gateway session boundary 14, browser-storage/public-surface 8, workbench identity guard 6), plus the copy-as-new identity-gate case in `editor-save.test.tsx` and the export typed-error regression in `export-dialog.test.tsx`. **Findings:** H-1 HIGH CLOSED (`e2fe708`) — the recorded NI-042 workbench cross-identity retention gap: `persistence/session-owner.ts` revalidates the verified session subject on mount/AUTH_CHANGE/cross-tab-storage/focus/visibility, a verified DIFFERENT principal unmounts the editor, clears the sessionStorage recovery draft and shows the honest session boundary, and the conflict dialog's copy-as-new path re-verifies the subject before creating anything (a verified 401 deliberately keeps the SAME owner's content for the NI-040 recovery contract — tradeoff recorded in the report; outages never clear; RLS 404 remains the write backstop). M-2 MEDIUM CLOSED (`ba30c06` hardening verified, assertion added `fd9ca9a`) — `GET/PATCH /api/me` now return `Cache-Control: no-store` at the API layer, asserted on the owner's own 200 responses. M-3 MEDIUM CLOSED (`efda125`) — the NI-063 finding's error-mapping half was a real defect: openapi-fetch consumes the non-ok body itself, so the export client's `response.json()` re-read always failed and degraded every typed refusal to the generic fallback; fixed by parsing the client-captured error object, with the specific `REVISION_NOT_ACKNOWLEDGED` copy pinned by regression. L-1 LOW CLOSED (`e7b543a`) — the recovered scanner rewrite exempted every generic-word-shaped server value (a weak signing secret could lose its scan); the exemption now applies to `SUPABASE_JWT_AUDIENCE` only, while the always-on self-test proves both directions on every run. P-1 LOW UNRESOLVED (does not block release) — the workbench pins `currentVersionId` at load only (NI-063 finding): exporting right after in-session saves gets the typed 409, which now renders its specific honest guidance; routed to the NI-058/NI-040 owners as NI-063 recorded (fixing it would have restructured the save pipeline inside a security audit). E-1 environmental: Linux-container runtime/staging evidence stays with NI-069. **WIP recovery disposition (`ba30c06`):** kept and executed — all five Python suites, both web suites, the discovery-list union, the `users/routes.py` no-store hardening and the scanner's self-test design; fixed — the scanner's over-broad generic-word exemption (L-1) and the web specs' wiring (the separate `e2e/security` vitest config was never collected by the standard run: the specs moved into the standard `tests/security` tree and the config was deleted, so `pnpm --filter web test` collects them while Playwright's `*.spec.ts` glob stays untouched — recorded decision per the dispatch instruction); the honest-boundary note and auth-change consumer inventory were updated to the closed state; the NI-063 gateway allowlist (892d877) and the recovered `e2e` harness coexist untouched. NI-070's release report/manifest (open PR branch, not in origin/main at audit time) were audited read-only: no credential/token/internal-URL shapes, no private document references; the automated public-surface scans apply to them generically at merge time.

Recheck actual staging-machine budgets under NI-067. No mobile viewport/device/touch pass is required or authorized in this scope.

**Owner ordering update — 11 November 2026 (NI-077):** before deployment, measure the same budgets and lazy mounting in a documented local production-mode environment. This is the local NI-064 acceptance under the new finish→local→deploy order; actual staging-machine budgets are rechecked in NI-067. This does not transfer the current claim or treat local results as staging evidence; all desktop accessibility criteria remain required.

**Known UI regression inputs from NI-024 browser review (8 November 2026):** sidebar `/workspace/data/*` and `/workspace/models` aliases prefetch 404 while actual implemented public routes are `/data/*` and `/models`; reconcile the canonical navigation/route aliases during full-flow QA. The unused `Button asChild` path supplies Radix Slot multiple children (spinner/null plus child), producing a runtime Children.only error; add a real slotted-link regression before claiming that primitive supported. Global unlayered anchor color can override layered primary-link foreground utilities; NI-024's Continue uses the existing on-accent token explicitly, and the shared color layering should be covered by the full accessibility/contrast pass.

- **Result:** implemented on `feat/ni-064-desktop-qa` (commits `5949a95` regression fixes, `6018661` a11y suites + fixes, `5c82459` perf measurements + QA report; QA report: [docs/qa/NI-064-desktop-qa-report.md](qa/NI-064-desktop-qa-report.md)). (1) **`apps/web/e2e/accessibility/`** — desktop 1440×900 Playwright suites over the NI-063 real-stack harness (`@axe-core/playwright` 4.14.0 added per DEPENDENCIES.md): axe scans of landing/thesis/methodology/`/data`/chart detail/`/models`/login and the workspace home/my-research/saved-charts plus the research editor with the 200-block/20-chart synthetic fixture document — ZERO serious/critical violations on every surface; keyboard journeys asserting actual focus targets (skip link first with the 2px focus ring drawn, Shift+Tab walk paper→outline→toolbar, insert menu open/navigate/insert with live announcement, slash menu open/insert/Escape, keyboard block reorder Mod-Alt-Arrows with announcement + order flip/restore, chart pinned-context `<details>` and source dialog with Escape focus return, Add-hypothesis and Export dialog traps + Escape-with-return); chart-data-without-hover checks (exact-values table = labeled keyboard-focusable region with every covered day's original decimal strings, keyboard range presets with URL round-trip, "Accessible summary" from the keyboard, editor chart node's embedded table). (2) **`apps/web/e2e/performance/`** — `*.perf.ts` LOCAL-ONLY config (does not match the journey config's `testMatch`, so CI/`test:e2e` are unaffected; the TESTING_STRATEGY staging-machine rule is the recorded why) measuring the PRD budgets on the real stack with the NI-064 fixtures. (3) **Harness fixtures:** a 366-day synthetic dataset staged/published through the real NI-028 pipeline BEFORE the journey dataset (so the NI-063 journey's "latest" assumptions hold; perf views pin the perf dataset by UUID), and the 200-block/20-liveChart-ref document created through the real principal bootstrap + document service with all 20 contexts frozen by the normal save path. (4) **Product fixes found by this QA (in-boundary, regression-tested):** (a) the slash-menu trigger NEVER fired in a real browser — prosemirror-view's DOM-diff path only calls `handleTextInput` when the edited parent already has a text child and the canonical empty document has no blocks at all, so the "/" trigger was dead in production; now listens for `beforeinput` (incl. the childless-document case) and `insertBlock` replaces the "/" trigger block so the slash never stays behind (`slash-menu.ts`, `commands.ts`; jsdom regression suite `tests/editor/slash-menu.test.ts` + the e2e typing path). (b) the Mod-Alt move / Mod-Shift-d duplicate / Mod-Shift-Backspace delete SHORTCUTS were silent for screen readers (only toolbar buttons announced) — new `editor-operation-announce` bridge carries the toolbar copy to the polite live region (`ui-bridge.ts`, `commands.ts`, `editor-shell.tsx`). (c) the Export dialog (opened from a plain controlled button, not a Radix trigger) did not return focus on Escape — explicit return-focus ref (`export-dialog.tsx`, `editor-shell.tsx`). (d) `/data` category cards failed axe `label-content-name-mismatch` (serious, 5 nodes) — accessible names now contain the visible text. (5) **The three recorded NI-024 regressions — all fixed with regression tests:** sidebar aliases → implemented `/data/*`+`/models` routes (registry + INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE.md reconciled; unit test forbids alias hrefs; e2e asserts every sidebar link resolves <400 on the production server); `Button asChild` renders exactly one slotted child (pending slotted links announce `aria-busy`, no spinner sibling; two unit regressions); the global anchor rule moved into the Tailwind v4 `base` cascade layer and the NI-024 inline-style workaround was removed (e2e asserts the Continue link's computed `text-on-accent`/accent background). Honest operator-side remainder: VoiceOver announcement quality, real IME composition, and physical-keyboard checks of the slash trigger (the e2e covers the same production `beforeinput` pipeline via `keyboard.insertText` because Playwright's synthetic `keyboard.type` bypasses Chromium's editing event pipeline on this build).

Budgets PASS with ~an order of magnitude headroom — no limit revision needed. 4) `pnpm --filter web test --maxWorkers=2` — **49 files / 518 tests pass** (510 baseline + 8 new). 5) `pnpm --filter web lint` — 0 errors/0 warnings; `pnpm --filter web typecheck` — clean; `pnpm --filter web build` — exit 0. 6) `node scripts/check-doc-links.mjs` — **669 links resolve**. 7) `uv lock --check --project apps/api` — OK (no backend source changes; the harness imports are run under the frozen API env). 8) `git diff --check` clean. 9) Dependency addition per DEPENDENCIES.md: `@axe-core/playwright` pinned **4.14.0** (npm `dist-tags.latest`, published 2026-10-15, MPL-2.0, peer `playwright-core >= 1.0.0` resolved against the existing 1.65.0; alternatives `axe-playwright` and manual axe-core injection rejected; lockfile diff contained to the axe subtree; choice recorded in DEPENDENCIES.md). 10) CI note: **no checks.yml change needed** — the existing `e2e` job runs `pnpm --filter web test:e2e`, whose config picks up `e2e/accessibility/*.spec.ts` (proven by the 17/17 combined run); the perf suite stays local-only by design (documented in the QA report and the perf config header).
