### NI-065 — Implement advisory integrity checks and optional AI tools

- **Epic:** 13 — Quality and Security
- **Priority:** P2
- **Dependencies:** NI-062, NI-063, NI-050
- **Complexity:** L
- **Status:** DONE
- **Claimed by:** ZCode implementation subagent, dispatched by coordinator session sess_9345ee4f-f534-4698-98d2-5809986ac4c3
- **Execution role:** A with B typed evidence-tool/API handoff
- **Started at:** 2026-11-12T16:54:30Z
- **Branch/worktree:** feat/ni-065-integrity; /Users/ilyutkinn/Desktop/near-intelligence-a30
- **Authorization:** Owner handoff v2 (docs/FINISH_BEFORE_DEPLOY_PROMPT.md, 12 November 2026) supersedes the former plan-only card and requires working tools; PR #88 carries the updated registry. External model use remains optional and separately configured/authorized.
- **Dependency verification:** NI-062, NI-063 and NI-050 are DONE in this registry; verified on docs/ni-077-handoff-v2 at commit 2787fd5.
- **Scope update:** Owner 2026-11-12 requires working tools before deployment; the former plan-only card is superseded. External model use remains optional and separately configured/authorized.

**Description:** Deliver local evidence assistance that verifies metadata deterministically and keeps interpretive suggestions advisory.

**Implementation steps:** Specify and implement typed checks for missing citations/pins, broken same-document evidence links and stale context. Add accessible editor findings with jump-to-source and rerun on the current acknowledged revision; do not rewrite scientific content automatically. Implement bounded evidence-tool contracts and an optional opt-in AI adapter for grounded commentary over an explicitly selected context, behind server configuration. Keep credentials server-only and external transmission off by default; no provider purchase or private document transmission without applicable permission. Record API/feature/ADR changes before implementation.

**Expected files/modules:** integrity ADR; deterministic evidence-check module/API; generated tool contracts; editor findings panel; optional provider adapter and grounding/security tests.

**Acceptance criteria:** Actual local document→checks→actionable finding→fix→rerun works without any AI key, with owner isolation and exact block/revision/context references. Suggestions cannot invent numeric results or change pins/coefficients; contradictions/causality are advisory with sources and no truth score. Optional configured AI tools are implemented and contract-tested; unavailable configuration is explicit, never a fake successful response. Core checks/editing/save/export remain usable without provider credentials or during adapter failure. No unapproved context leaves the machine; real provider connectivity is not claimed from a fixture. Documentation alone cannot close this card.

**Testing requirements:** Known missing/valid metadata oracle cases; two-user/context boundary; stale revision and source-jump browser flow; untrusted document/prompt-injection/evidence-grounding/secret tests; adapter success/failure with isolated test transport; editing/export unaffected by AI outage. Real external smoke only with existing explicit destination/context/provider permission; otherwise record the optional integration limit.

- **Result (2026-11-12, this dispatch):** Implemented on `feat/ni-065-integrity` (six commits on top of the handoff base 2787fd5: claim+ADR fd4f244, contracts 09b424f, implementation+tests b356209, fixes 1e7b5bf, editor panel 090d273, docs b34f557). **(1) ADR-022** (docs/DECISIONS.md) records the deterministic-checks + optional-adapter boundary with the amendment-record fields (context, decision, alternatives, scientific/security effect, affected contracts, migration/rollback, verification, date); no database migration is needed — checks read existing `document_versions`/registry/dataset rows only. **(2) Deterministic integrity checks** — new `apps/api/src/near_api/integrity/` (checks.py + routes.py): read-only, owner-scoped `GET /api/documents/{document_id}/integrity` (optional historical `version_id` labeled `is_acknowledged:false`, never refused; an owned document without a revision returns the explicit empty report). Deterministic per revision content — no wall-clock field; a rerun is a new GET; no finding mutates anything. Checks cover ONLY post-save reality (ADR-022): `stale_chart_context` (newer approved dataset for the pinned chart's required metrics via the NI-032 `resolve_latest_version` order; a not-ready data plane yields NO finding — missing is not zero), `missing_chart_context`/`invalid_chart_context` (corruption signals, reported never swallowed), `evidence_target_unavailable`/`insight_context_unavailable` (public model-run/citation drift, read-only re-run of the save-time registry membership rule), `chart_definition_unavailable`/`dataset_pin_unavailable` (out-of-band registry divergence; the runtime roles are grant/trigger-blocked from causing it, which is exactly why the divergence must be surfaced), and `uncited_pinned_chart`/`uncited_pinned_dataset_table` (pinned data with no evidence link and no insight carrying its context; an insight IS a source-bearing annotation citing through its frozen context — the precise definition is documented in the ADR, EDITOR_SPECIFICATION and API_SPECIFICATION). Broken same-document links are deliberately NOT re-checked (save-time blocked, cannot happen). Findings carry advisory/warning severities (never a truth score), exact block/entity ids, dataset/definition ids, `context_hash`, human messages and a `jump` target; report bounded at 400 findings by construction (200 blocks × ≤2). **(3) Optional grounded-commentary adapter** — new `apps/api/src/near_api/ai/` (adapter.py + settings.py + routes.py): `POST /api/documents/{document_id}/grounded-commentary` accepts ONLY explicitly selected block ids (≤10) + a bounded question; the prompt is built ONLY from those blocks (frozen contexts + bounded authored prose; unselected private content is never included — unit-tested) under server-authored rules (content is DATA, never instructions; numbers only as verbatim read-backs marked quoted; no truth score). Provider call reuses the finalized-header refresh bounds EXACTLY (one httpx.Client, injectable transport, `follow_redirects=False`, `trust_env=False`, 512 KiB byte cap, 60 s timeout, typed failures, provider error bodies/URLs never surfaced, no retry, one attempt); documented OpenAI/Anthropic response shapes, anything else typed `unsupported_provider_response`. Grounding is enforced deterministically: whole numeric tokens in the response that the selected context does not contain are refused as `ungrounded_commentary` (safe-side). Settings: frozen `AICommentarySettings` in `ai/settings.py` (auth_settings.py pattern) — `NEAR_AI_COMMENTARY_ENABLED=false` default; while disabled the served config carries `None` for every provider field (the key is never read into memory in served form) and the endpoint answers the typed `unavailable`/`adapter_disabled` state; enabled-but-incomplete config is a readable `SettingsError` surfacing as `adapter_misconfigured`. The response contract forbids faking success (`advisory: Literal[True]`, `ok` requires items + no reason, `unavailable` names the disabled adapter, `failed` requires a typed reason). The route is read-only; the transaction closes BEFORE the network call. **(4) Contracts** — `schemas/integrity.py` models registered in the OpenAPI components (`INTEGRITY_CONTRACT_MODELS`), generated openapi.json/api.ts committed; `pnpm check:contracts` green. **(5) Editor findings panel** — `apps/web/src/features/editor/integrity/` (api.ts + integrity-panel.tsx) mounted in the editor-shell tools aside AND tools drawer next to the NI-050 evidence panel; ready/revision-gated fetch rerunning when the acknowledged revision changes; honest loading/empty ("No integrity findings on this revision")/error+retry/unsaved-edits states; jump-to-source via setNodeSelection+focus+scrollIntoView with the announcement pattern; findings rendered as a labeled list with severity labels, exact visible references (block/entity/definition/dataset/newest-dataset/context-hash shortIds, no hover-only data), stale findings explicitly stating the refresh stays the chart block's own action; aria-live polite status; jest-axe clean. Same-origin gateway allowlist gains `integrity` (GET, optional `version_id` UUID query) and `grounded-commentary` (POST, no query). **(6) Docs** — ADR-022, EDITOR_SPECIFICATION "Advisory integrity findings (NI-065)" section, API_SPECIFICATION two endpoint rows + four reusable schema rows + implemented notes, FEATURE_SPECIFICATIONS F18 implementation note, TESTING_STRATEGY advisory-surface note.

**Description:** Create immutable app images and a fresh-machine onboarding path without auto-starting collection.

**Implementation steps:** Add production Dockerfiles/web/API/export runtime; finish Compose profiles/env validation/readiness; include Node renderer and Python locks; restrict volumes/network/roles; document alternative native setup; implement the real clean-clone sequence in docs/JUDGE_SETUP.md using frozen Python/frontend locks, auth/config/migrations and an explicitly permitted input bundle.

**Expected files/modules:** Dockerfiles; docker-compose.yml; deployment/; README.md

**Acceptance criteria:** Fresh setup boots/migrates with documented commands; exact images reproducible; collectors off by default; API internal and persistent artifacts mounted; judge setup is tested on Linux/container runtime, not inferred from wheel availability.

**Testing requirements:** Clean build/Compose smoke; readiness/migration failure; inspect image secrets and renderer availability; scheduler disabled check.

- **Result:** Portable runtimes implemented on `feat/ni-066-portable-runtimes` (commits `96dccee` implementation+docs on top of the claim commit `2f64137`). (1) `Dockerfile.web` — multi-stage Next.js production image: corepack-activated pinned pnpm 12.16.1, `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`, `next build`, then a runtime stage with a PROD-ONLY dependency tree (separate `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --prod` stage; no dev deps, no caches), non-root `node` user, `/health`-based HEALTHCHECK (200 healthy, readable 503 unhealthy), `next start` on :3000. (2) `Dockerfile.api` — ONE Python image serving FOUR entry commands (api HTTP service, one-shot Alembic migrate, export worker CLI, bounded refresh CLI): `uv sync --frozen` from `apps/api/uv.lock` (uv 0.12.13 image, no hash stripping, `UV_PYTHON_PREFERENCE=only-system` on python:3.13-slim), plus the packaged NI-057 Node renderer — the render script, the three option-builder/format/view-types TS modules and the resolved `echarts`/`zrender`/`tslib`/`zod` trees are extracted from a frozen workspace install into the preserved repo layout (`/app/apps/web/scripts/render-charts.mjs`, `/app/node_modules`) so the module-location default path and Node's upward resolution both hit packaged paths; `NEAR_CHART_RENDERER_NODE=/usr/local/bin/node` and `NEAR_CHART_RENDERER_SCRIPT=/app/apps/web/scripts/render-charts.mjs` pinned as image ENV seams; type-only imports (generated shared-types, echarts option types) are erased and deliberately not packaged; node binary copied from the pinned node image (same bookworm base), verified running; non-root uid 10001. Packaging decision recorded: one Python image instead of a separate worker image (a second copy of the locked scientific stack for no isolation gain); the renderer is spawned only by the export worker subprocess, never by the HTTP services. (3) `docker-compose.yml` (extends NI-003's DB-only definition to the full deployment): default `up -d` = db + api + web ONLY; `migrate`, `worker`, `refresh` are explicit one-shot `run --rm` services behind profiles — nothing about collection auto-starts (no image entrypoint or service runs ingestion; the refresh service runs as `near_operator` and refuses with exit 3 before any request while the allowlist is empty, which it is in every fresh environment). Env: api/web read app config from the ignored root `.env` via `env_file`; container-facing `DATABASE_URL`/`MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL` are rebuilt from the four DB role secrets with the internal `db` hostname, so localhost URLs never enter containers; worker gets only its two DB URLs; provider credentials reach no one-shot service. Networks/ports: single internal bridge network; db/api/web publish to `127.0.0.1` only (`${DB_PORT:-5432}`/`${API_HOST_PORT:-8000}`/`${WEB_HOST_PORT:-3000}`); volumes restricted to `near_db` (PG 18 data) + `near_artifacts` (refresh/worker storage root); `restart: "no"` everywhere; images pinned by digest including `postgres:18.6-bookworm`. (4) `.dockerignore` — build context excludes `.env*` (keeping `.env.example`), `research/`, `product-plan/`, docs, local state, dependency trees and test suites; verified by exporting the exact context (`FROM scratch + COPY . /ctx` via buildx `--output type=local`): the context contains only `apps/`, `packages/`, root manifests, the Dockerfiles, compose file, `requirements.txt` and `apps/api/README.md` (hatchling needs it); no `.env`, no `research/`, no `product-plan/`, no `node_modules`, no `.git`. (5) `deployment/README.md` — packaging decisions, live registry digest checks, credential/role matrix, context-boundary test procedure. (6) `.env.example` — `OPERATOR_DATABASE_URL` placeholder added for native worker/refresh runs (near_operator lease credential path, NI-058). (7) `docs/JUDGE_SETUP.md` rewritten as the REAL tested sequence; README run instructions consolidated (no duplication — links). Honest scope: the authenticated export journey was verified on the native runtime (not through the compose containers end-to-end); Linux container execution remains NI-069's release-rehearsal gate; NI-067 untouched (no CI image-build job added — existing checks.yml zones belong to other tasks, and the local build verification stands in for it; recorded as skipped deliberately).
